HFT visibility is governed by the authoring policy above.","docket_mode":"quarantine_without_hft","docket_ready":true}},"selected_context_units":[],"support_registry":[{"branch_refs":["root_000622/B001","root_000622/B005"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:4","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_056032c38556e65a20b5","text":"4. **Expulsion from the social center (exploratory).** `ز ب ن:B005` supplies distance or withdrawal away from one’s dwellings, while 96:17 invokes `ناديه`, the actor’s assembly place and people. Combined with `ز ب ن:B001` pushing away, the branch can assign the called force a spatial result: removal from the council-space on which the actor relies. I infer this displacement from the opposed place and repulsion images. The ayah may therefore threaten loss of social refuge as well as physical defeat.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":[],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:retrospective_surprises_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_190172acb3625cac073b","text":"96:19 makes the counter-call serve the worshipper's freedom rather than becoming the final focus. The surprise is that enforcement clears space for prostration and nearness; it is not the endpoint.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000622/B001"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-2","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_28403d09d7de1eabea41","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"96:18:2\",\"root_000622\",[\"B001\"]]],\"grade\":\"strong\",\"text\":\"Çağrılanlar, taşkın baskıyı ve onun topluluğunu geri itip ibadet edenin itaatsizlik, secde ve yaklaşma alanını açan savunucu karşı basınçtır.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":["root_000622/B001"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:2","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_480d267a150055666c52","text":"2. **Repelling force as the agents’ defining function.** `ز ب ن:B001` supplies pushing away, collision, prevention, and defensive resistance. The preceding actor has denied, turned away, faced forelock seizure, and tried to mobilize a council. The branch assigns `الزبانية` a functional role: they push back the transgressive pressure and prevent its coalition from controlling the scene. The packet names the force but not each action; I infer the enforcement direction from the confrontation. The noun becomes a dynamic counter-pressure rather than an unexplained group label.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000622/B004"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-3","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_4837e795529606fec5ec","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"96:18:2\",\"root_000622\",[\"B004\"]]],\"grade\":\"weak\",\"text\":\"Akrep kıskacı çağrışımı, çağrılan güce perçemden yakalama sahnesini tamamlayan kavrayıcı ve geri itici bir beden imgesi ekler.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":["root_000478/B001"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_7969eb41487cbfdb8f4e","text":"1. **A superior counter-mobilization.** `د ع و:B001` supplies calling to draw near. The future `سندع` answers the immediately preceding jussive `فليدع`, but the objects differ: the actor calls his council, while the answering speaker calls `الزبانية`. The repeated verb makes the two mobilizations formally comparable; the changed subject and object establish asymmetry. I infer that the second call counters and supersedes the first. The ayah reads as a response in the same social grammar but with greater enforcement capacity.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000622/B005","root_000622/B007"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-5","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_82f38682ef7bbe44e9e8","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"96:18:2\",\"root_000622\",[\"B005\",\"B007\"]]],\"grade\":\"reject\",\"text\":\"Uzaklaştırma ve kimsenin kalmaması çağrışımları, karşı gücün kişiyi dayandığı toplantı merkezinden çıkarıp toplumsal sığınağını boşaltabileceği ihtimalini açar.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":["root_000622/B001"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:retrospective_surprises_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_8c205bf509901039f852","text":"96:19 revealed that the repelling call is not merely a terminal threat: it changes the action conditions so refusal, prostration, and approach become practicable. That later command sharpened `ز ب ن:B001` as defensive counter-pressure and supported the inference that the called force blocks the antagonist’s coalition rather than simply adding another crowd.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000622/B008"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:3","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_a5a40ae23f7f2bc8e19a","text":"3. **A second bodily hold completing the forelock image.** `ز ب ن:B008` supplies the neck as a point that can be seized. Forelock seizure in 96:15 has already located control at the front of the head. The neck-hold branch extends that arrest topology: called repellers can immobilize the body whose leading point is already threatened. I infer coordination between the two accepted bodily images; the packet does not narrate a second seizure. The line therefore activates a complete restraint mechanism around head and neck.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000622/B008"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-4","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_db3f4bec8074aaa025f4","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"96:18:2\",\"root_000622\",[\"B008\"]]],\"grade\":\"weak\",\"text\":\"Boyundan tutma çağrışımı, daha önce perçemden hedef alınan bedenin baş ve boyun çevresinde tümüyle hareketsiz bırakılabileceği ikinci bir yakalama imgesi kurar.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":["root_000478/B001"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-1","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_e8ec061776017cd9e1f6","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"96:18:1\",\"root_000478\",[\"B001\"]]],\"grade\":\"strong\",\"text\":\"Aynı çağırma fiilinin ilahî özneyle tekrarlanması, insan topluluğunun seferberliğine aynı toplumsal dilde fakat üstün güçle verilen karşılıktır.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":["root_000478/B001","root_000478/B004","root_000622/B001","root_000622/B004","root_000622/B005"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_e9e61076a09e11359c0a","text":"1. The divine counter-call summons repelling enforcers. Evidence: `د ع و B001` calling/drawing near; `د ع و B004` calling down an unwelcome thing; `ز ب ن B001` pushing, repelling, collision, prevention. Structural cue: 96:17 `فليدع` is answered by 96:18 `سندع`, matching the verb while changing speaker and object. Mechanism: the human call draws a council; the divine call draws forces of repulsion. Abductive move: the packet supplies the mirrored call and repelling branch; I infer judicial counter-summons. Reading change: the ayah reads as exact escalation: summons against summons, council against enforcers. 2. `الزبانية` also activates the scorpion-claw image. Evidence: `ز ب ن B004` paired horns/claws of the scorpion, alongside `س ف ع B001` seizure and `ن ص ي B001` forelock. Structural cue: after the forelock threat, the called agents have a root inventory including grasping/repelling appendages. Mechanism: the false front that tried to control worship is met by claw-like repulsion/control. Abductive move: the packet supplies scorpion-claw imagery; I infer a vivid secondary embodiment of enforcement. Reading change: the called agents are not abstract “helpers”; they embody repulsive, gripping force. 3. The no-one/distance branches create a social reversal. Evidence: `ز ب ن B005` distance from dwellings, `ز ب ن B007` no one there, contrasted with `ن د و B001` council gathering. Structural cue: the human summons presumes populated backing; the divine response can empty or distance that social space. Mechanism: the council's presence is neutralized by a call that drives away, distances, and leaves no protective company. Abductive move: the packet supplies council presence and z-b-n absence/distance; I infer a social-emptying effect. Reading change: the ayah reads as stripping the opponent's assembly of protective force.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"anchor_evidence":[],"anchor_evidence_coverage":{"boundary":"Exact Arabic verifies surface contact only.
